However, there is a known, long-standing issue in the library where this functionality does not work as expected.\n\n### The Issue\nEven when you set `drawable.eraseOnClick: false` in your configuration, the underlying `drawClear` function is often triggered regardless of that setting. Specifically, the library\'s internal logic (found in `src/drag.ts`) often executes a clear operation when you click on an empty square or a square with a piece that does not match the current turn color. Because this behavior is deeply embedded in the event handling and `drawClear` is not exposed in the API, it is currently difficult to override or disable this behavior through standard configuration.\n\n### Current Status\n*   **Known Bug:** This has been documented by the community as a bug (e.g., [Issue #322](https://github.com/lichess-org/chessground/issues/322)).\n*   **No Reliable Workaround:** As of August 2026, there is no official fix or configuration flag that successfully disables this "erase on click" behavior in all scenarios. \n*   **Development Activity:** While developers have experimented with `eraseOnClick: false` in test boards within the `chessground-examples` repository, the core library issue remains unresolved in the main codebase.\n\n### Summary of Behavior\n*   **Expected:** `eraseOnClick: false` should prevent shapes from disappearing when clicking the board.\n*   **Actual:** Shapes are frequently cleared when clicking empty squares
